## 鉁?鍔熻兘浜粸
- **🐛 修復點擊桌面觸發自動隱藏 (1.2.9)**：全螢幕偵測現在排除 Windows 桌面視窗（Progman/WorkerW），點擊桌面不再誤觸發靈動島自動隱藏。
- **🎨 修復自動隱藏後重新顯示邊緣被裁切 (1.2.9)**：顯示前先恢復視窗尺寸與外觀，再在 Loaded 優先級重新定位，確保兩側圓角完整不再被截斷。
- **⚡ 效能與記憶體最佳化 (1.2.8)**：修復 8 處事件訂閱洩漏；UpdateVisibility 僅在值實際變化時觸發通知（減少 ~25 次無效通知）；畫刷屬性快取（消除每幀分配）；封面取色快取（避免展開/收起時重複取樣）；歌詞畫刷快取；低功耗波形真正降幀至 30fps；GPU 取樣快取；時鐘文字去重。
- **🔧 修復展開時的白色/黑色方框 (1.2.7)**: 玻璃層現與卡片圓角同步裁剪，展開時不再露出直角邊緣（淺色模式白框 / 深色模式黑框），視覺更乾淨。

- **馃幎 Smoother karaoke highlighting (1.2.4)**: character-by-character progress now uses smoothstep easing with cross-fades between letters 鈥?the highlight flows continuously left to right like a light band, easing in and out instead of stepping abruptly.
- **馃攱 Persistent low-battery indicator (1.2.4)**: when the battery drops below the threshold and is not charging, a small battery pill stays in the island鈥檚 top-right corner (red 鈮?0% / orange otherwise), refreshing in real time; it disappears automatically when plugged in or recovered, and can be toggled in settings.
- **馃帶 Device connection animation (1.2.4)**: Bluetooth connect/disconnect now shows an iOS-style animated card; on connect, the device鈥檚 battery is read automatically and shown as 鈥淒evice name 路 Battery xx%鈥?(name only when battery can鈥檛 be read), without blocking the UI.
- **馃帳 Adjustable lyric style (1.2.3)**: customize normal/current-line font size, line spacing, karaoke advance speed, and highlight/base colors; changes apply instantly and the standalone lyrics window follows.
- **馃搵 Notification history (1.2.3)**: review recent notifications at the bottom of the expanded island, click to replay, configure the max count, and clear with one click.
- **馃敂 Push & notification polish (1.2.2)**: collapsed alerts show a single line (icon + title + one-line summary) and truncate when long, so they no longer widen the island; the island auto-widens for long notifications so right-side widgets and text stay fully visible.
## English

# WinIslands 鈥?Dynamic Island for Windows

- **🐛 Fixed desktop click triggering auto-hide (1.2.9)**: Full-screen detection now excludes Windows desktop windows (Progman/WorkerW); clicking the desktop no longer falsely triggers island auto-hide.
- **🎨 Fixed edge clipping after auto-hide re-show (1.2.9)**: ShowIsland now restores window size and appearance before showing, then repositions at Loaded priority, ensuring rounded corners are fully intact.
> **Bring the iOS Dynamic Island to Windows 11 / 10 鈥?a modern, multi-functional Dynamic Island widget for Windows.**
> Built with **.NET 8 + WPF**, free and open source (MIT), **no ads 路 no telemetry 路 no data collection**.

馃寪 Website: https://WinIslands.JudeKwong.com 锝?GitHub: https://github.com/JudeKwong/WinIslands
- **🐛 Fixed desktop click triggering auto-hide (1.2.9)**: Full-screen detection now excludes Windows desktop windows (Progman/WorkerW); clicking the desktop no longer falsely triggers island auto-hide.
- **🎨 Fixed edge clipping after auto-hide re-show (1.2.9)**: ShowIsland now restores window size and appearance before showing, then repositions at Loaded priority, ensuring rounded corners are fully intact.

---

## 鉁?Highlights
- **🐛 Fixed desktop click triggering auto-hide (1.2.9)**: Full-screen detection now excludes Windows desktop windows (Progman/WorkerW); clicking the desktop no longer falsely triggers island auto-hide.
- **🎨 Fixed edge clipping after auto-hide re-show (1.2.9)**: ShowIsland now restores window size and appearance before showing, then repositions at Loaded priority, ensuring rounded corners are fully intact.
- **⚡ Performance & memory optimization (1.2.8)**: Fixed 8 event handler leaks; UpdateVisibility only fires PropertyChanged when values actually change (~25 fewer unnecessary notifications); cached brush properties (eliminates per-access allocations); cached cover color sampling (skips redundant render-to-bitmap on expand/collapse); cached lyric brushes; low-power wave timer truly reduces to 30fps; cached GPU PerformanceCounterCategory; clock text deduplication.
- **馃┕ Fixed white/black frame when expanded (1.2.7)**: The glass layer now matches the card corner radius with clipping, so rectangular edges no longer peek out when expanding (white frame in light mode / black frame in dark mode) 鈥?a cleaner look.
- **馃Ч Hide clipboard widget while media plays (1.2.5)**: When collapsed, the copy icon and clipboard history number no longer appear beside the lyrics 鈥?a cleaner, tidier island.
- **鈿?Full 60fps animation optimization (1.2.5)**: Audio waveform rendering raised to 60fps; card content width is fixed during expand/collapse animations to avoid per-frame re-layout 鈥?smoother, more fluid motion.
- **馃幎 Smoother karaoke highlighting (1.2.4)**: character-by-character progress now uses smoothstep easing with cross-fades between letters 鈥?the highlight flows continuously left to right like a light band, easing in and out instead of stepping abruptly.
- **馃攱 Persistent low-battery indicator (1.2.4)**: when the battery drops below the threshold and is not charging, a small battery pill stays in the island鈥檚 top-right corner (red 鈮?0% / orange otherwise), refreshing in real time; it disappears automatically when plugged in or recovered, and can be toggled in settings.
- **馃帶 Device connection animation (1.2.4)**: Bluetooth connect/disconnect now shows an iOS-style animated card; on connect, the device鈥檚 battery is read automatically and shown as 鈥淒evice name 路 Battery xx%鈥?(name only when battery can鈥檛 be read), without blocking the UI.
- **馃帳 Adjustable lyric style (1.2.3)**: customize normal/current-line font size, line spacing, karaoke advance speed, and highlight/base colors; changes apply instantly and the standalone lyrics window follows.
- **馃搵 Notification history (1.2.3)**: review recent notifications at the bottom of the expanded island, click to replay, configure the max count, and clear with one click.

- **馃寠 Buttery-smooth motion (1.2.1)**: iOS-style spring damping + staggered content transitions for expand/collapse; linked volume-change animation; smart opacity layering between compact and expanded; cross-fade cover transitions; smooth light/dark theme interpolation; per-frame animation performance optimizations.

- **鈻?Media playback control**: Native integration with Windows global media sessions (SMTC), compatible with NetEase Cloud Music, QQ Music, Spotify, Apple Music, Groove, Movies & TV and more; plus dedicated support for the Cider local API; falls back to window-title detection when unavailable. Album artwork, draggable seek bar, play/pause/next/previous 鈥?all included; when multiple players are open you can switch the control source with one click.
- **鈾?Karaoke word-by-word lyrics**: The expanded card scrolls with synchronized highlighting and word-by-word illumination; four-tier source priority: local `.lrc` 鈫?AMLL TTML 鈫?player lyrics API 鈫?optional online lyrics. Bilingual lyrics, translation toggle, one-click copy of the current line; per-song lyric time fine-tuning, and a standalone lyrics window with adjustable opacity and locking.
- **鈻?Customizable widget system**: 30+ widgets 鈥?time, weather, date (with lunar calendar/solar terms), CPU/GPU/RAM/disk, network speed, battery, input method, quick toggles (WiFi/Bluetooth/night mode/mute), etc. Each widget supports a custom icon, checkbox selection and drag-to-reorder, with one-line/multi-line modes.
- **馃彎 Island API**: A local HTTP / WebSocket interface that lets any third-party software push information to the Dynamic Island in real time (like iOS third-party App Island integration). Supports images, animated progress, heartbeat renewal, light/dark themes, button actions and input fields; pushes never change the island's width or cover other widgets.
- **鉁?Event animations**: Bluetooth connect/disconnect, charging start/done, low battery, network restored, calendar/RSS/email reminders and more are shown on the island with elegant animations.
- **鉁?Appearance & motion**: 18 theme presets, custom accent color, liquid-glass acrylic, wallpaper color extraction, marquee, 4 motion skins (iOS spring etc.), 4 audio-wave styles (pulsing with the music); non-linear easing for expand/collapse at a smooth 60 fps; PerMonitorV2 high-DPI support.
- **馃柋 Interaction & intelligence**: unlock-to-drag with edge snapping, auto-hide on fullscreen/lock screen, double-click and middle-click quick actions, quick-action buttons, file transfer station (drag files onto the island and out to other apps), smart DND while screen-recording, scheduled light/dark theme switching.
- **鈿?Productivity & automation**: Pomodoro timer, to-dos, clipboard history, quick launcher, schedule reminders; meeting mute assistant, screen-record/screenshot hints, file-copy/download progress on the island; global hotkeys and a rules engine.
- **馃洝 Privacy & security**: no telemetry, no ads, no data uploads. Fully offline except for user-enabled online lyrics/weather; all configuration and data stays local in `%APPDATA%\WinIslands`.
- **馃攣 One-tap lyric-source switching**: cycle instantly between Auto / Local LRC / AMLL TTML / Cider API / Online lyrics, and the current song's lyrics reload right away
- **馃洝锔?Automatic crash recovery**: after an abnormal exit, the next launch auto-notifies recovery 鈥?no more hangs, black screens, or lost state
- **鈴憋笍 Adjustable animation duration**: a new expand/collapse animation-duration slider (300鈥?400ms) lets you fine-tune speed to your liking

## 馃敡 Building

### Requirements
- Windows 10 1809+ / Windows 11
- .NET 8 SDK

### Build & test
```powershell
dotnet build WinIslands.slnx -c Release
dotnet test  WinIslands.slnx -c Release
```

### Publish
```powershell
# Self-contained (includes the .NET 8 runtime, no install needed)
.uild\publish.ps1

# Framework-dependent (small, requires .NET 8 Desktop Runtime)
.uild\publish.ps1 -FrameworkDependent
```
Output goes to `publish\win-x64\`. For stable releases, place the build under `releases\<version>\win-x64\` and rename it with the version number.

### Installer (optional)
Install [Inno Setup 6](https://jrsoftware.org/isinfo.php), then run `iscc.exe build\release-<version>.iss` to produce `releases\<version>\WinIslands-Setup-<version>.exe` (universal installer; auto-installs x64 / ARM64 by architecture).

---

## 馃殌 Usage

1. Launch `WinIslands.exe` (startup with Windows is optional); a tray icon appears. Closing the main window does not quit the process 鈥?it just minimizes to the tray.
2. Play any music: NetEase Cloud Music, QQ Music, Spotify, Apple Music official app, etc. appear automatically through the system media session; Cider 鈥?see below; other players fall back to window-title detection.
3. **Click** the island to expand the full card (hovering does not expand): draggable seek, playback controls, volume, synced lyrics; click again to collapse.
4. Tray menu: show/hide, standalone lyrics window, start with Windows, Do Not Disturb, check for updates, view logs, settings, quit.
5. Global hotkeys (all customizable): `Ctrl+Alt+P` play/pause 路 `Ctrl+Alt+鈫?鈫抈 previous/next 路 `Ctrl+Alt+I` show/hide 路 `Ctrl+Alt+Space` expand/collapse 路 `Ctrl+Space` quick launcher 路 `Ctrl+Alt+V` clipboard history panel.
6. Useful command-line arguments: `--demo` demo mode 路 `--diagnose` writes a diagnostic report 路 `--settings` opens settings at startup.

### Cider integration
1. Enable "Allow external control" in Cider settings.
2. WinIslands Settings 鈫?Media 鈫?enable Cider (port auto-detected from `10767` with a local scan, or set manually).
3. While playing, the island source shows `Cider`; you can display artwork/progress/lyrics and control play, seek and volume.

Config file: `%APPDATA%\WinIslands\settings.json` (JSON; changes in the settings UI apply instantly, export/import supported).

| Key | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `Language` | `zh-CN` | UI language: `zh-CN` / `en-US` |
| `Position` | `Center` | Position: `Center` top-center / `Right` top-right |
| `Monitor` | `Primary` | Monitor: `Primary` / `All` / `Index` |
| `MonitorIndex` | `0` | Monitor number when `Monitor=Index` |
| `OffsetX` / `OffsetY` | `0` / `8` | Position offset (px) |
| `Opacity` | `0.92` | Opacity 0.3鈥?.0 |
| `Theme` | `Auto` | `Auto` / `Light` / `Dark` |
| `ThemePreset` | `Default` | Theme preset (18 presets + Custom) |
| `AccentColor` | `#6C5CE7` | Accent color `#RRGGBB` |
| `ThemeScheduledEnabled` | `false` | Scheduled light/dark switching (only when `Theme=Auto`) |
| `ThemeScheduleDarkStartHour` / `EndHour` | `19` / `7` | Dark-period start/end hours |
| `FontScale` / `CornerRadius` | `1.0` / `28` | Font scale 0.8鈥?.4 / corner radius 16鈥?0 |
| `AnimationStyle` | `Spring` | Motion skin: `Spring` / `Soft` / `Elastic` / `Fade` |
| `CompactWidth` / `CompactHeight` | `360` / `72` | Compact width / height |
| `CompactWidthAuto` / `CompactHeightAuto` | `true` | Auto-fit compact size |
| `ExpandedWidth` / `MaxExpandedHeight` | `400` / `384` | Expanded width / max expanded height |
| `HideWhenNoMedia` | `true` | Hide the island when no media is playing |
| `IslandAlwaysVisible` | `false` | Keep the island always visible (widgets shown without media) |
| `ShowWhenPaused` | `true` | Keep showing while paused |
| `StartWithWindows` | `false` | Start with Windows |
| `IsLocked` | `true` | Locked (drag only after unlocking) |
| `EdgeSnapEnabled` | `true` | Snap to screen edges/center when released |
| `FullScreenAutoHideEnabled` | `true` | Auto-hide on fullscreen |
| `LockScreenAutoHideEnabled` | `true` | Auto-hide on lock screen (Win+L / RDP disconnect), restore on unlock |
| `SingleLineMode` | `true` | One-line mode: all widgets on one row in compact state |
| `DoubleClickAction` | `PlayPause` | Double-click quick action |
| `MiddleClickAction` | `PlayPause` | Middle-click quick action |
| `CiderEnabled` / `CiderPort` | `true` / `0` | Cider local API (0 = auto-detect) |
| `OnlineLyricsEnabled` | `true` | Online lyrics toggle (right-click the island; unofficial API, mind copyright) |
| `AmllTtmlEnabled` | `true` | AMLL word-by-word lyrics (api.amll.dev, unofficial) |
| `KaraokeHighlight` | `true` | Word-by-word karaoke highlighting |
| `StandaloneLyricsWindow` | `false` | Standalone lyrics window |
| `UseSystemVolume` | `true` | Use system volume for non-Cider sources |
| `LowBatteryThreshold` | `20` | Low-battery alert threshold (%), 0 = off |
| `DoNotDisturbManual` | `false` | Manual Do Not Disturb |
| `DoNotDisturbEnabled` | `false` | Scheduled Do Not Disturb |
| `DoNotDisturbStartHour/Minute` | `22` / `0` | DND start (minute precision) |
| `DoNotDisturbEndHour/Minute` | `8` / `0` | DND end (minute precision) |
| `IslandApiEnabled` / `IslandApiPort` | `true` / `9840` | Island API switch / port |
| `IslandApiToken` | `""` | Optional island API token |
| `IslandApiDefaultDuration` | `30` | Default island display duration (s) |
| `WaveStyle` | `Bars` | Audio wave style: `Bars` / `Spectrum` / `Ring` / `Particles` |
| `WidgetOrder` | `Time,Weather,...` | Widget order (comma-separated keys) |
| `Components` | object | Per-widget "no song / playing" two-column checkboxes |
| `MediaApps` | `[]` | Media app enable/disable and priority |
| `Rules` | `[]` | Automation rules (conditions + actions) |
| `ReduceMotion` | `false` | Reduce motion (accessibility/battery) |
| `GlobalHotkeysEnabled` | `true` | Global hotkeys switch |
| `LowPowerMode` | `false` | Low-power mode: lower frame rate when idle, simplified animations |

## 馃彎 Island API (third-party integration)

Any software can push information to the Dynamic Island through the local HTTP / WebSocket interface, similar to iOS third-party App "Island" integration.

| Method | Path | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| POST | `/v1/island/push` | Push/update a card |
| POST | `/v3/island/push` | v1 superset: image / animated progress / heartbeat |
| PATCH | `/v3/island/push/{id}` | Partial update |
| DELETE | `/v1/island/push/{id}` | Remove a card |
| GET | `/v1/island/active` | Query the active card |
| GET | `/v3/ws` | WebSocket two-way channel |
| GET | `/v1/health` | Health check |

Supports: title/body/icon/subtitle, progress, buttons (open link / launch app / run command / notify callback), input fields, images, animated progress, heartbeat renewal, light/dark themes, custom accent color and a priority queue. **Pushes never change the island's width.**

Full documentation: [docs/IslandAPI.md](docs/IslandAPI.md)

---

## 馃洝 Privacy & security

- **No telemetry, no ads, no uploads**. The app makes no network requests except user-enabled "online lyrics" and "weather".
- The only networked scenarios: Cider artwork download, AMLL word-by-word lyrics (api.amll.dev), user-enabled online lyrics and weather (Open-Meteo).
- All data is stored locally in `%APPDATA%\WinIslands\`; logs only record local runtime information.

---

## 鈿狅笍 Known limitations

- Word-by-word karaoke depends on the lyrics source and progress; it highlights per character when AMLL TTML / LRC timing exists, otherwise falls back to whole-line highlighting.
- Players occasionally report regressing progress (e.g. Cider/SMTC momentarily reporting 0): a position guard ignores momentary regressions.
- SMTC coverage depends on whether the player registers a global media session; some old players can only be detected via window title (no control buttons).
- Cider 1.x (old API) is not supported; only 2.x and above.
- Online lyrics / AMLL / weather are unofficial interfaces and may break as versions change.

---

## 鉂?FAQ

**Q: The island does not appear?**
Make sure media is playing; `HideWhenNoMedia` is enabled by default, so hiding without media is expected. Run `--diagnose` to see the session list.

**Q: Cider shows "Not connected"?**
Make sure "Allow external control" is enabled in Cider settings, check the port (default 10767), and confirm Cider is enabled in WinIslands settings.

**Q: The tray icon is still there after quitting?**
Use Tray menu 鈫?Quit; closing the island window only hides it (tray-resident by design).

## 馃殌 Uso

1. Inicia `WinIslands.exe` (opcional: iniciar con Windows); aparece un icono en la bandeja. Cerrar la ventana principal no cierra el proceso: solo minimiza a la bandeja.
2. Reproduce cualquier m煤sica: NetEase Cloud Music, QQ Music, Spotify, la app oficial de Apple Music, etc., aparecen autom谩ticamente mediante la sesi贸n multimedia del sistema; Cider, ver m谩s abajo; otros reproductores usan detecci贸n por t铆tulo de ventana.
3. **Haz clic** en la isla para expandir la tarjeta completa (pasar el cursor no expande): seek arrastrable, controles de reproducci贸n, volumen, letras sincronizadas; haz clic de nuevo para contraer.
4. Men煤 de la bandeja: mostrar/ocultar, ventana de letras independiente, iniciar con Windows, No molestar, buscar actualizaciones, ver registros, ajustes, salir.
5. Atajos globales (todos personalizables): `Ctrl+Alt+P` reproducir/pausar 路 `Ctrl+Alt+鈫?鈫抈 anterior/siguiente 路 `Ctrl+Alt+I` mostrar/ocultar 路 `Ctrl+Alt+Space` expandir/contraer 路 `Ctrl+Space` lanzador r谩pido 路 `Ctrl+Alt+V` panel del historial del portapapeles.
6. Argumentos de l铆nea de comandos 煤tiles: `--demo` modo demostraci贸n 路 `--diagnose` escribe un informe de diagn贸stico 路 `--settings` abre ajustes al inicio.

### Integraci贸n con Cider
1. Activa "Allow external control" en los ajustes de Cider.
2. Ajustes de WinIslands 鈫?Media 鈫?activa Cider (puerto autodetectado desde `10767` con escaneo local, o config煤ralo manualmente).
3. Mientras se reproduce, la fuente de la isla muestra `Cider`; puedes ver portada/progreso/letras y controlar reproducci贸n, seek y volumen.

## 馃彎 API Island (integraci贸n de terceros)

Cualquier software puede enviar informaci贸n al Dynamic Island mediante la interfaz HTTP/WebSocket local, similar a la integraci贸n "Island" de apps de terceros en iOS.

| M茅todo | Ruta | Descripci贸n |
| --- | --- | --- |
| POST | `/v1/island/push` | Enviar/actualizar una tarjeta |
| POST | `/v3/island/push` | Superconjunto de v1: imagen / progreso animado / heartbeat |
| PATCH | `/v3/island/push/{id}` | Actualizaci贸n parcial |
| DELETE | `/v1/island/push/{id}` | Eliminar una tarjeta |
| GET | `/v1/island/active` | Consultar la tarjeta activa |
| GET | `/v3/ws` | Canal bidireccional WebSocket |
| GET | `/v1/health` | Comprobaci贸n de salud |

Soporta: t铆tulo/cuerpo/icono/subt铆tulo, progreso, botones (abrir enlace / iniciar app / ejecutar comando / devoluci贸n de llamada notify), campos de entrada, im谩genes, progreso animado, renovaci贸n por heartbeat, temas claro/oscuro, color de acento personalizado y cola de prioridad. **Los env铆os nunca cambian el ancho de la isla.**

Documentaci贸n completa: [docs/IslandAPI.md](docs/IslandAPI.md)

## 馃洝 Privacidad y seguridad

- **Sin telemetr铆a, sin anuncios, sin cargas**. La app no hace solicitudes de red salvo las "letras en l铆nea" y el "clima" habilitados por el usuario.
- Las 煤nicas situaciones con red: descarga de portadas de Cider, letras palabra por palabra AMLL (api.amll.dev), letras en l铆nea y clima habilitados por el usuario (Open-Meteo).
- Todos los datos se almacenan localmente en `%APPDATA%\WinIslands\`; los registros solo guardan informaci贸n local de ejecuci贸n.

---

## 鈿狅笍 Limitaciones conocidas

- El karaoke palabra por palabra depende de la fuente de letras y el progreso; resalta por car谩cter cuando existe temporizaci贸n AMLL TTML / LRC; si no, vuelve al resaltado por l铆nea completa.
- A veces los reproductores informan progreso hacia atr谩s (p. ej. Cider/SMTC reportando 0 moment谩neamente): un guardi谩n de posici贸n ignora las regresiones moment谩neas.
- La cobertura SMTC depende de si el reproductor registra una sesi贸n multimedia global; algunos reproductores antiguos solo se detectan por t铆tulo de ventana (sin botones de control).
- Cider 1.x (API antigua) no es compatible; solo 2.x y superiores.
- Las letras en l铆nea / AMLL / el clima son interfaces no oficiales y pueden fallar cuando cambian las versiones.

## 鉂?Preguntas frecuentes

**P: 驴La isla no aparece?**
Aseg煤rate de que haya medios reproduci茅ndose; `HideWhenNoMedia` est谩 activado por defecto, as铆 que ocultarse sin medios es lo esperado. Ejecuta `--diagnose` para ver la lista de sesiones.

**P: 驴Cider muestra "No conectado"?**
Aseg煤rate de que "Allow external control" est茅 activado en los ajustes de Cider, comprueba el puerto (por defecto 10767) y confirma que Cider est茅 habilitado en los ajustes de WinIslands.

**P: 驴El icono de la bandeja sigue ah铆 despu茅s de salir?**
Usa Men煤 de la bandeja 鈫?Salir; cerrar la ventana de la isla solo la oculta (dise帽o residente en la bandeja).

- **鉁?Apparence et animation** : 18 th猫mes pr茅d茅finis, couleur d'accent personnalis茅e, acrylique verre liquide, extraction de couleur du fond d'茅cran, d茅filement d茅filant, 4 peaux d'animation (ressort iOS, etc.), 4 styles d'onde audio (palpitant au rythme de la musique) ; easing non lin茅aire pour d茅plier/replier 脿 60 fps fluides ; prise en charge haute DPI PerMonitorV2.
- **馃柋 Interaction et intelligence** : d茅verrouillage pour glisser avec magn茅tisme aux bords, masquage automatique en plein 茅cran / sur l'茅cran de verrouillage, actions rapides double-clic et clic central, boutons d'action rapide, station de transfert de fichiers (glisser des fichiers vers l'卯le puis vers d'autres applications), Ne pas d茅ranger intelligent pendant l'enregistrement d'茅cran, bascule programm茅e du th猫me clair/sombre.
- **鈿?Productivit茅 et automatisation** : minuteur Pomodoro, t芒ches, historique du presse-papiers, lanceur rapide, rappels d'agenda ; assistant de mise en sourdine en r茅union, alertes d'enregistrement d'茅cran / de capture, progression de copie/t茅l茅chargement de fichiers sur l'卯le ; raccourcis globaux et moteur de r猫gles.
- **馃洝 Confidentialit茅 et s茅curit茅** : aucune t茅l茅m茅trie, aucune publicit茅, aucune remont茅e de donn茅es. Enti猫rement hors ligne sauf paroles en ligne et m茅t茅o activ茅es manuellement ; toute la configuration et les donn茅es restent locales dans `%APPDATA%\WinIslands`.
- **馃攣 Changement de source des paroles en un clic** : basculez instantan茅ment entre Auto / LRC local / AMLL TTML / API Cider / Paroles en ligne, et les paroles de la chanson en cours sont recharg茅es aussit么t
- **馃洝锔?R茅cup茅ration automatique apr猫s un plantage** : apr猫s une fermeture anormale, le prochain lancement notifie la r茅cup茅ration 鈥?plus de blocages, d'茅cran noir ni d'茅tat perdu

## 馃敡 Compilation

### Pr茅requis
- Windows 10 1809+ / Windows 11
- SDK .NET 8

### Compiler et tester
```powershell
dotnet build WinIslands.slnx -c Release
dotnet test  WinIslands.slnx -c Release
```

### Publier
```powershell
# Autonome (contient le runtime .NET 8, aucune installation requise)
.\build\publish.ps1

# D茅pendant du framework (l茅ger, n茅cessite .NET 8 Desktop Runtime)
.\build\publish.ps1 -FrameworkDependent
```
Les artefacts se trouvent dans `publish\win-x64\`. Les versions stables sont plac茅es dans `releases\<version>\win-x64\` et renomm茅es selon le num茅ro de version.

### Installeur (facultatif)
Installez [Inno Setup 6](https://jrsoftware.org/isinfo.php), puis ex茅cutez `iscc.exe build\release-<version>.iss` pour g茅n茅rer `releases\<version>\WinIslands-Setup-<version>.exe` (installeur universel, installe x64 / ARM64 selon l'architecture).

## 馃殌 Utilisation

1. Lancez `WinIslands.exe` (d茅marrage automatique configurable) ; l'ic么ne appara卯t dans la barre d'茅tat ; fermer la fen锚tre principale ne quitte pas le processus (r茅duit dans la barre d'茅tat).
2. Lisez de la musique : NetEase Cloud Music, QQ Music, Spotify, Apple Music officiel, etc. s'affichent automatiquement via la session multim茅dia syst猫me ; pour Cider voir ci-dessous ; les autres lecteurs utilisent la d茅tection par titre de fen锚tre.
3. **Cliquez** sur l'卯le pour d茅plier la carte compl猫te (le survol ne d茅plie pas) : seek par glissement de la progression, contr么le de lecture, volume, paroles synchronis茅es ; recliquez pour replier.
4. Menu de la barre d'茅tat : afficher/masquer, fen锚tre de paroles ind茅pendante, d茅marrage automatique, Ne pas d茅ranger, rechercher les mises 脿 jour, voir les journaux, param猫tres, quitter.
5. Raccourcis globaux (tous personnalisables) : `Ctrl+Alt+P` lecture/pause 路 `Ctrl+Alt+鈫?鈫抈 pr茅c茅dent/suivant 路 `Ctrl+Alt+I` afficher/masquer 路 `Ctrl+Alt+Espace` d茅plier/replier 路 `Ctrl+Espace` lanceur rapide 路 `Ctrl+Alt+V` panneau de l'historique du presse-papiers.
6. Arguments de ligne de commande courants : `--demo` mode d茅mo 路 `--diagnose` rapport de diagnostic 路 `--settings` ouvre les param猫tres au d茅marrage.

### Int茅gration Cider
1. Dans les param猫tres de Cider, activez 芦 Autoriser le contr么le externe 禄 (Allow external control).
2. Param猫tres WinIslands 鈫?M茅dia 鈫?activer Cider (port d茅tect茅 automatiquement `10767` et scan local, ou saisie manuelle).
3. Pendant la lecture, la source affich茅e sur l'卯le est `Cider` : pochette/progression/paroles, contr么le lecture, seek, volume.

## 馃彎 API Island (int茅gration tierce)

Tout logiciel peut pousser des informations vers le Dynamic Island via l'interface HTTP / WebSocket locale, comme l'int茅gration 芦 Island 禄 des apps tierces sur iOS.

| M茅thode | Chemin | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| POST | `/v1/island/push` | Pousser / mettre 脿 jour une carte |
| POST | `/v3/island/push` | Sur-ensemble de v1 : image / progression anim茅e / heartbeat |
| PATCH | `/v3/island/push/{id}` | Mise 脿 jour partielle |
| DELETE | `/v1/island/push/{id}` | Retirer une carte |
| GET | `/v1/island/active` | Interroger la carte active |
| GET | `/v3/ws` | Canal WebSocket bidirectionnel |
| GET | `/v1/health` | Contr么le de sant茅 |

Prend en charge : titre/corps/ic么ne/sous-titre, progression, boutons (ouvrir un lien / lancer un programme / ex茅cuter une commande / rappel notify), champs de saisie, images, progression anim茅e, renouvellement par heartbeat, th猫mes clair/sombre, couleur d'accent personnalis茅e, file de priorit茅. **L'envoi ne modifie pas la largeur de l'卯le**.

Documentation compl猫te : [docs/IslandAPI.md](docs/IslandAPI.md)

## 馃洝 Confidentialit茅 et s茅curit茅

- **Aucune t茅l茅m茅trie, aucune publicit茅, aucune remont茅e**. Sauf 芦 paroles en ligne 禄 et 芦 m茅t茅o 禄 activ茅es manuellement, l'application ne fait aucune requ锚te r茅seau.
- Seules connexions r茅seau : t茅l茅chargement des pochettes Cider, paroles mot 脿 mot AMLL (api.amll.dev), paroles en ligne et m茅t茅o apr猫s activation (Open-Meteo).
- Toutes les donn茅es sont stock茅es localement dans `%APPDATA%\WinIslands\` ; les journaux n'enregistrent que des informations d'ex茅cution locales.

---

## 鈿狅笍 Limites connues

- Le karaok茅 mot 脿 mot d茅pend de la source et de la progression des paroles : avec une timeline mot 脿 mot AMLL TTML / LRC, mise en 茅vidence par mot, sinon repli sur la mise en 茅vidence de la phrase enti猫re.
- Certains lecteurs signalent parfois une progression qui recule (ex. Cider/SMTC signalent bri猫vement 0) : une garde de position ignore les retours instantan茅s.
- La couverture SMTC d茅pend de l'enregistrement de la session multim茅dia globale par le lecteur ; certains anciens lecteurs ne sont d茅tect茅s que par titre de fen锚tre (sans boutons de contr么le).
- Cider 1.x (ancienne API) non adapt茅, uniquement 2.x et sup茅rieur.
- Paroles en ligne / AMLL / m茅t茅o sont des interfaces non officielles, susceptibles de cesser de fonctionner.

**Q : L'ic么ne de la barre d'茅tat reste apr猫s la sortie ?**
Menu de la barre d'茅tat 鈫?Quitter ; fermer la fen锚tre de l'卯le ne fait que la masquer (l'卯le reste r茅sidente par conception).

## 馃敡 丕賱亘賳丕亍

### 丕賱賲鬲胤賱亘丕鬲
- 賵賷賳丿賵夭 10 1809+ / 賵賷賳丿賵夭 11
- .NET 8 SDK

### 丕賱亘賳丕亍 賵丕賱丕禺鬲亘丕乇
```powershell
dotnet build WinIslands.slnx -c Release
dotnet test  WinIslands.slnx -c Release
```

### 丕賱賳卮乇
```powershell
# 賲爻鬲賯賱 (賷鬲囟賲賳 賵賯鬲 鬲卮睾賷賱 .NET 8貙 亘丿賵賳 鬲孬亘賷鬲)
.\build\publish.ps1

# 賲毓鬲賲丿 毓賱賶 丕賱廿胤丕乇 (禺賮賷賮貙 賷鬲胤賱亘 .NET 8 Desktop Runtime)
.\build\publish.ps1 -FrameworkDependent
```
丕賱賲賱賮丕鬲 丕賱賳丕鬲噩丞 賮賷 `publish\win-x64\`. 鬲賵囟毓 丕賱廿氐丿丕乇丕鬲 丕賱賲爻鬲賯乇丞 賮賷 `releases\<丕賱廿氐丿丕乇>\win-x64\` 賵鬲購毓丕丿 鬲爻賲賷鬲賴丕 丨爻亘 乇賯賲 丕賱廿氐丿丕乇.

### 丕賱賲孬亘賾鬲 (丕禺鬲賷丕乇賷)
孬亘賾鬲 [Inno Setup 6](https://jrsoftware.org/isinfo.php)貙 孬賲 賳賮賾匕 `iscc.exe build\release-<丕賱廿氐丿丕乇>.iss` 賱鬲賵賱賷丿 `releases\<丕賱廿氐丿丕乇>\WinIslands-Setup-<丕賱廿氐丿丕乇>.exe` (賲孬亘賾鬲 卮丕賲賱貙 x64 / ARM64 丨爻亘 丕賱亘賳賷丞).

## 馃殌 胤乇賷賯丞 丕賱丕爻鬲禺丿丕賲

1. 卮睾賾賱 `WinIslands.exe` (賷賲賰賳 囟亘胤 丕賱鬲卮睾賷賱 賲毓 亘丿亍 鬲卮睾賷賱 賵賷賳丿賵夭)貨 賷馗賴乇 乇賲夭 賮賷 毓賱亘丞 丕賱賳馗丕賲貨 廿睾賱丕賯 丕賱賳丕賮匕丞 丕賱乇卅賷爻賷丞 賱丕 賷購賳賴賷 丕賱毓賲賱賷丞 鈥?賷購氐睾賾乇 廿賱賶 丕賱毓賱亘丞 賮賯胤.
2. 卮睾賾賱 兀賷 賲賵爻賷賯賶: NetEase Cloud Music 賵QQ Music 賵Spotify 賵Apple Music 丕賱乇爻賲賷 賵睾賷乇賴丕 鬲馗賴乇 鬲賱賯丕卅賷賸丕 毓亘乇 噩賱爻丞 丕賱賵爻丕卅胤貨 兀賲丕 Cider 賮丕賳馗乇 兀丿賳丕賴貨 丕賱賲卮睾賾賱丕鬲 丕賱兀禺乇賶 鬲購賰鬲卮賮 毓亘乇 毓賳賵丕賳 丕賱賳丕賮匕丞.
3. **丕賳賯乇** 毓賱賶 丕賱噩夭賷乇丞 賱鬲賲丿賷丿 丕賱亘胤丕賯丞 丕賱賰丕賲賱丞 (丕賱鬲賲乇賷乇 賱丕 賷賲丿賾丿): 爻丨亘 丕賱鬲賯丿賾賲 賱賱丕賳鬲賯丕賱 (seek)貙 丕賱鬲丨賰賲 賮賷 丕賱鬲卮睾賷賱貙 賲爻鬲賵賶 丕賱氐賵鬲貙 賰賱賲丕鬲 賲鬲夭丕賲賳丞貨 丕賳賯乇 賲噩丿丿賸丕 賱賱胤賷賾.
4. 賯丕卅賲丞 丕賱毓賱亘丞: 廿馗賴丕乇/廿禺賮丕亍貙 賳丕賮匕丞 賰賱賲丕鬲 賲爻鬲賯賱丞貙 丕賱鬲卮睾賷賱 賲毓 賵賷賳丿賵夭貙 芦賱丕 鬲購夭毓噩禄貙 丕賱亘丨孬 毓賳 丕賱鬲丨丿賷孬丕鬲貙 毓乇囟 丕賱爻噩賱丕鬲貙 丕賱廿毓丿丕丿丕鬲貙 禺乇賵噩.
5. 丕禺鬲氐丕乇丕鬲 毓丕賲丞 (賰賱賴丕 賯丕亘賱丞 賱賱鬲禺氐賷氐): `Ctrl+Alt+P` 鬲卮睾賷賱/廿賷賯丕賮 路 `Ctrl+Alt+鈫?鈫抈 丕賱爻丕亘賯/丕賱鬲丕賱賷 路 `Ctrl+Alt+I` 廿馗賴丕乇/廿禺賮丕亍 路 `Ctrl+Alt+賲爻丕賮丞` 鬲賲丿賷丿/胤賷賾 路 `Ctrl+賲爻丕賮丞` 賲卮睾賾賱 爻乇賷毓 路 `Ctrl+Alt+V` 賱賵丨丞 爻噩賱 丕賱丨丕賮馗丞.
6. 賵爻丕卅胤 爻胤乇 丕賱兀賵丕賲乇 丕賱卮丕卅毓丞: `--demo` 賵囟毓 丕賱毓乇囟 路 `--diagnose` 鬲賯乇賷乇 鬲卮禺賷氐賷 路 `--settings` 賮鬲丨 丕賱廿毓丿丕丿丕鬲 毓賳丿 丕賱廿賯賱丕毓.

### 鬲賰丕賲賱 Cider
1. 賮賷 廿毓丿丕丿丕鬲 Cider 賮毓賾賱 芦丕賱爻賲丕丨 亘丕賱鬲丨賰賲 丕賱禺丕乇噩賷禄 (Allow external control).
2. 廿毓丿丕丿丕鬲 WinIslands 鈫?丕賱賵爻丕卅胤 鈫?鬲賮毓賷賱 Cider (丕賱賲賳賮匕 賷購賰鬲卮賮 鬲賱賯丕卅賷賸丕 `10767` 賵賷購賮丨氐 賲丨賱賷賸丕貙 兀賵 賷購丿禺賱 賷丿賵賷賸丕).
3. 兀孬賳丕亍 丕賱鬲卮睾賷賱貙 丕賱賲氐丿乇 毓賱賶 丕賱噩夭賷乇丞 賴賵 `Cider`: 丕賱睾賱丕賮/丕賱鬲賯丿賾賲/丕賱賰賱賲丕鬲 賵丕賱鬲丨賰賲 賵seek 賵賲爻鬲賵賶 丕賱氐賵鬲.

## 鈿欙笍 卮乇丨 丕賱廿毓丿丕丿丕鬲

賲賱賮 丕賱廿毓丿丕丿丕鬲: `%APPDATA%\WinIslands\settings.json` (JSON貨 丕賱鬲睾賷賷乇丕鬲 賮賷 丕賱賵丕噩賴丞 鬲購胤亘賻賾賯 賮賵乇賸丕貙 賲毓 廿賲賰丕賳賷丞 丕賱鬲氐丿賷乇/丕賱丕爻鬲賷乇丕丿).

| 丕賱賲賮鬲丕丨 | 丕賱丕賮鬲乇丕囟賷 | 丕賱賵氐賮 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `Language` | `zh-CN` | 賱睾丞 丕賱賵丕噩賴丞: `zh-CN` / `en-US` |
| `Position` | `Center` | 丕賱賲賵囟毓: `Center` 兀毓賱賶 丕賱賲賳鬲氐賮 / `Right` 兀毓賱賶 丕賱賷賲賷賳 |
| `Monitor` | `Primary` | 丕賱卮丕卮丞: `Primary` 丕賱乇卅賷爻賷丞 / `All` 賰賱 丕賱卮丕卮丕鬲 / `Index` 卮丕卮丞 賲禺鬲丕乇丞 |
| `MonitorIndex` | `0` | 乇賯賲 丕賱卮丕卮丞 丕賱賲禺鬲丕乇丞 |
| `OffsetX` / `OffsetY` | `0` / `8` | 廿夭丕丨丞 丕賱賲賵囟毓 (亘賰爻賱) |
| `Opacity` | `0.92` | 丕賱卮賮丕賮賷丞 0.3鈥?.0 |
| `Theme` | `Auto` | `Auto` / `Light` / `Dark` |
| `ThemePreset` | `Default` | 爻賲丞 丕賱賲馗賴乇 (18 爻賲丞 噩丕賴夭丞 + Custom) |
| `AccentColor` | `#6C5CE7` | 賱賵賳 丕賱鬲賲賷賷夭 `#RRGGBB` |
| `ThemeScheduledEnabled` | `false` | 鬲亘丿賷賱 賮丕鬲丨/丿丕賰賳 賲噩丿賵賱 (賷毓賲賱 賲毓 `Theme=Auto` 賮賯胤) |
| `ThemeScheduleDarkStartHour` / `EndHour` | `19` / `7` | 爻丕毓丕鬲 亘丿亍/丕賳鬲賴丕亍 丕賱賮鬲乇丞 丕賱丿丕賰賳丞 |
| `FontScale` / `CornerRadius` | `1.0` / `28` | 賲賯賷丕爻 丕賱禺胤 0.8鈥?.4 / 賳氐賮 賯胤乇 丕賱夭賵丕賷丕 16鈥?0 |
| `AnimationStyle` | `Spring` | 兀爻賱賵亘 丕賱丨乇賰丞: `Spring` / `Soft` / `Elastic` / `Fade` |
| `CompactWidth` / `CompactHeight` | `360` / `72` | 丕賱胤賵賱 / 丕賱丕乇鬲賮丕毓 丕賱賲囟睾賵胤 |
| `CompactWidthAuto` / `CompactHeightAuto` | `true` | 囟亘胤 鬲賱賯丕卅賷 賱賱丨噩賲 丕賱賲囟睾賵胤 |
| `ExpandedWidth` / `MaxExpandedHeight` | `400` / `384` | 丕賱胤賵賱 丕賱賲賲丿丿 / 兀賯氐賶 丕乇鬲賮丕毓 賲賲丿丿 |
| `HideWhenNoMedia` | `true` | 廿禺賮丕亍 丕賱噩夭賷乇丞 毓賳丿 毓丿賲 鬲卮睾賷賱 賵爻丕卅胤 |
| `IslandAlwaysVisible` | `false` | 丕賱噩夭賷乇丞 丿丕卅賲丞 丕賱馗賴賵乇 (鬲毓乇囟 丕賱兀丿賵丕鬲 丨鬲賶 亘丿賵賳 賵爻丕卅胤) |
| `ShowWhenPaused` | `true` | 丕賱廿馗賴丕乇 毓賳丿 丕賱廿賷賯丕賮 丕賱賲丐賯鬲 |
| `StartWithWindows` | `false` | 丕賱鬲卮睾賷賱 賲毓 亘丿亍 鬲卮睾賷賱 賵賷賳丿賵夭 |
| `IsLocked` | `true` | 賲賯賮賱丞 (亘毓丿 賮鬲丨 丕賱賯賮賱 賷賲賰賳 賱賱賲丕賵爻 丕賱爻丨亘) |
| `EdgeSnapEnabled` | `true` | 丕賱丕賱鬲氐丕賯 丕賱鬲賱賯丕卅賷 亘丕賱丨丕賮丞/丕賱賲賳鬲氐賮 毓賳丿 丕賱廿賮賱丕鬲 |
| `FullScreenAutoHideEnabled` | `true` | 丕賱廿禺賮丕亍 丕賱鬲賱賯丕卅賷 賮賷 賲賱亍 丕賱卮丕卮丞 |
| `LockScreenAutoHideEnabled` | `true` | 廿禺賮丕亍 鬲賱賯丕卅賷 毓賱賶 卮丕卮丞 丕賱賯賮賱 (Win+L / 賮氐賱 爻胤丨 丕賱賲賰鬲亘 丕賱亘毓賷丿)貙 賵丕賱丕爻鬲毓丕丿丞 亘毓丿 廿賱睾丕亍 丕賱賯賮賱 |
| `SingleLineMode` | `true` | 賵囟毓 爻胤乇 賵丕丨丿: 賰賱 丕賱兀丿賵丕鬲 賮賷 爻胤乇 賵丕丨丿 賮賷 丕賱丨丕賱丞 丕賱賲囟睾賵胤丞 |
| `DoubleClickAction` | `PlayPause` | 賮毓賱 爻乇賷毓 亘丕賱賳賯乇丞 丕賱賲夭丿賵噩丞 毓賱賶 丕賱噩夭賷乇丞 |
| `MiddleClickAction` | `PlayPause` | 賮毓賱 爻乇賷毓 亘賳賯乇丞 丕賱賵爻胤 毓賱賶 丕賱噩夭賷乇丞 |
| `CiderEnabled` / `CiderPort` | `true` / `0` | 賵丕噩賴丞 Cider 丕賱賲丨賱賷丞 (0 = 賰卮賮 鬲賱賯丕卅賷) |
| `OnlineLyricsEnabled` | `true` | 丕賱賰賱賲丕鬲 毓亘乇 丕賱廿賳鬲乇賳鬲 (賯丕亘賱 賱賱鬲亘丿賷賱 亘丕賱賳賯乇 丕賱兀賷賲賳 毓賱賶 丕賱噩夭賷乇丞貨 賵丕噩賴丞 睾賷乇 乇爻賲賷丞貙 丕賳鬲亘賴 賱丨賯賵賯 丕賱賳卮乇) |
| `AmllTtmlEnabled` | `true` | 賰賱賲丕鬲 AMLL 賰賱賲丞賸 賰賱賲丞 (api.amll.dev貙 睾賷乇 乇爻賲賷) |
| `KaraokeHighlight` | `true` | 廿亘乇丕夭 賰丕乇賷賵賰賷 賰賱賲丞賸 賰賱賲丞 |
| `StandaloneLyricsWindow` | `false` | 賳丕賮匕丞 賰賱賲丕鬲 賲爻鬲賯賱丞 |
| `UseSystemVolume` | `true` | 氐賵鬲 丕賱賳馗丕賲 賱賱賲氐丕丿乇 睾賷乇 Cider |
| `LowBatteryThreshold` | `20` | 丨丿 鬲賳亘賷賴 丕賳禺賮丕囟 丕賱亘胤丕乇賷丞 (侏)貙 0 = 廿賷賯丕賮 |
| `DoNotDisturbManual` | `false` | 芦賱丕 鬲購夭毓噩禄 賷丿賵賷 |
| `DoNotDisturbEnabled` | `false` | 芦賱丕 鬲購夭毓噩禄 賲噩丿賵賱 (廿卮毓丕乇丕鬲 氐丕賲鬲丞 丨爻亘 丕賱賮鬲乇丞) |
| `DoNotDisturbStartHour/Minute` | `22` / `0` | 亘丿丕賷丞 芦賱丕 鬲購夭毓噩禄 (亘丿賯丞 丕賱丿賯賷賯丞) |
| `DoNotDisturbEndHour/Minute` | `8` / `0` | 賳賴丕賷丞 芦賱丕 鬲購夭毓噩禄 (亘丿賯丞 丕賱丿賯賷賯丞) |
| `IslandApiEnabled` / `IslandApiPort` | `true` / `9840` | 賲賮鬲丕丨 / 賲賳賮匕 賵丕噩賴丞 Island |
| `IslandApiToken` | `""` | 乇賲夭 丕禺鬲賷丕乇賷 賱賵丕噩賴丞 Island |
| `IslandApiDefaultDuration` | `30` | 賲丿丞 丕賱毓乇囟 丕賱丕賮鬲乇丕囟賷丞 (孬賵丕賳賺) |
| `WaveStyle` | `Bars` | 賳賲胤 賲賵噩丞 丕賱氐賵鬲: `Bars` / `Spectrum` / `Ring` / `Particles` |
| `WidgetOrder` | `Time,Weather,...` | 鬲乇鬲賷亘 丕賱兀丿賵丕鬲 (賲賮丕鬲賷丨 賲賮氐賵賱丞 亘賮賵丕氐賱) |
| `Components` | 賰丕卅賳 | 禺丕賳丕鬲 芦亘丿賵賳 賲賵爻賷賯賶 / 賲毓 賲賵爻賷賯賶禄 賱賰賱 兀丿丕丞 |
| `MediaApps` | `[]` | 鬲賮毓賷賱/鬲毓胤賷賱 賵兀賵賱賵賷丞 丕賱賲卮睾賾賱丕鬲 |
| `Rules` | `[]` | 賯賵丕毓丿 丕賱兀鬲賲鬲丞 (卮乇胤 + 賮毓賱) |
| `ReduceMotion` | `false` | 鬲賯賱賷賱 丕賱丨乇賰丞 (廿鬲丕丨丞/鬲賵賮賷乇 丕賱胤丕賯丞) |
| `GlobalHotkeysEnabled` | `true` | 賲賮鬲丕丨 丕賱丕禺鬲氐丕乇丕鬲 丕賱毓丕賲丞 |
| `LowPowerMode` | `false` | 賵囟毓 丕賱胤丕賯丞 丕賱賲賳禺賮囟丞: 禺賮囟 丕賱賲毓丿賱 賮賷 丕賱禺賲賵賱 賵鬲亘爻賷胤 丕賱丨乇賰丕鬲 |

## 馃彎 賵丕噩賴丞 Island (鬲賰丕賲賱 丕賱兀胤乇丕賮 丕賱禺丕乇噩賷丞)

賷賲賰賳 賱兀賷 亘乇賳丕賲噩 丿賮毓 丕賱賲毓賱賵賲丕鬲 廿賱賶 丕賱丿賷賳丕賲賷賰 丌賷賱丕賳丿 毓亘乇 賵丕噩賴丞 HTTP / WebSocket 丕賱賲丨賱賷丞貙 賲孬賱 鬲賰丕賲賱 Island 賱鬲胤亘賷賯丕鬲 iOS 丕賱禺丕乇噩賷丞.

| 丕賱胤乇賷賯丞 | 丕賱賲爻丕乇 | 丕賱賵氐賮 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| POST | `/v1/island/push` | 丿賮毓 / 鬲丨丿賷孬 亘胤丕賯丞 |
| POST | `/v3/island/push` | 賮丕卅賯 賱賭 v1: 氐賵乇丞 / 鬲賯丿賾賲 賲鬲丨乇賰 / heartbeat |
| PATCH | `/v3/island/push/{id}` | 鬲丨丿賷孬 噩夭卅賷 |
| DELETE | `/v1/island/push/{id}` | 廿夭丕賱丞 亘胤丕賯丞 |
| GET | `/v1/island/active` | 丕賱丕爻鬲毓賱丕賲 毓賳 丕賱亘胤丕賯丞 丕賱賳卮胤丞 |
| GET | `/v3/ws` | 賯賳丕丞 WebSocket 孬賳丕卅賷丞 丕賱丕鬲噩丕賴 |
| GET | `/v1/health` | 賮丨氐 丕賱氐丨丞 |

鬲丿毓賲: 丕賱毓賳賵丕賳/丕賱賳氐/丕賱兀賷賯賵賳丞/丕賱毓賳賵丕賳 丕賱賮乇毓賷貙 賵丕賱鬲賯丿賾賲貙 賵丕賱兀夭乇丕乇 (賮鬲丨 乇丕亘胤 / 鬲卮睾賷賱 亘乇賳丕賲噩 / 鬲賳賮賷匕 兀賲乇 / 乇丿 notify)貙 賵丨賯賵賱 丕賱廿丿禺丕賱貙 賵丕賱氐賵乇貙 賵丕賱鬲賯丿賾賲 丕賱賲鬲丨乇賰貙 賵鬲噩丿賷丿 heartbeat貙 賵丕賱爻賲丕鬲 丕賱賮丕鬲丨丞/丕賱丿丕賰賳丞貙 賵賱賵賳 鬲賲賷賷夭 賲禺氐氐貙 賵賯丕卅賲丞 兀賵賱賵賷丕鬲. **丕賱丿賮毓 賱丕 賷睾賷賾乇 毓乇囟 丕賱噩夭賷乇丞**.

丕賱鬲賵孬賷賯 丕賱賰丕賲賱: [docs/IslandAPI.md](docs/IslandAPI.md)

---

## 馃洝 丕賱禺氐賵氐賷丞 賵丕賱兀賲丕賳

- **賱丕 鬲鬲亘毓貙 賱丕 廿毓賱丕賳丕鬲貙 賱丕 乇賮毓 亘賷丕賳丕鬲**. 亘丕爻鬲孬賳丕亍 芦丕賱賰賱賲丕鬲 毓亘乇 丕賱廿賳鬲乇賳鬲禄 賵芦丕賱胤賯爻禄 丕賱賲賮毓賾賱賷賳 賷丿賵賷賸丕貙 賱丕 賷賯賵賲 丕賱鬲胤亘賷賯 亘兀賷 胤賱亘丕鬲 卮亘賰丞.
- 爻賷賳丕乇賷賵賴丕鬲 丕賱丕鬲氐丕賱 丕賱賵丨賷丿丞: 鬲賳夭賷賱 兀睾賱賮丞 Cider貙 賰賱賲丕鬲 AMLL 賰賱賲丞賸 賰賱賲丞 (api.amll.dev)貙 賵丕賱賰賱賲丕鬲 毓亘乇 丕賱廿賳鬲乇賳鬲 賵丕賱胤賯爻 亘毓丿 丕賱鬲賮毓賷賱 (Open-Meteo).
- 噩賲賷毓 丕賱亘賷丕賳丕鬲 賲丨賱賷丞 賮賷 `%APPDATA%\WinIslands\`貨 丕賱爻噩賱丕鬲 鬲爻噩賾賱 賲毓賱賵賲丕鬲 丕賱鬲卮睾賷賱 丕賱賲丨賱賷丞 賮賯胤.

---

## 鈿狅笍 丕賱賯賷賵丿 丕賱賲毓乇賵賮丞

- 丕賱賰丕乇賷賵賰賷 賰賱賲丞賸 賰賱賲丞 賷毓鬲賲丿 毓賱賶 賲氐丿乇 丕賱賰賱賲丕鬲 賵丕賱鬲賯丿賾賲: 賲毓 禺胤 夭賲賳賷 賰賱賲丞賸 賰賱賲丞 賲賳 AMLL TTML / LRC 賷鬲賲 丕賱廿亘乇丕夭 賰賱賲丞賸 賰賱賲丞貙 賵廿賱丕 賷購爻鬲禺丿賲 廿亘乇丕夭 丕賱噩賲賱丞 賰丕賲賱丞.
- 亘毓囟 丕賱賲卮睾賾賱丕鬲 賯丿 鬲購亘賱睾 兀丨賷丕賳賸丕 亘鬲賯丿賾賲 賲鬲乇丕噩毓 (賲孬賱 廿亘賱丕睾 Cider/SMTC 亘丕賱氐賮乇 賱丨馗賷賸丕): 丨丕乇爻 丕賱賲賵囟毓 賷鬲噩丕賴賱 丕賱鬲乇丕噩毓丕鬲 丕賱賱丨馗賷丞.
- 鬲睾胤賷丞 SMTC 鬲毓鬲賲丿 毓賱賶 鬲爻噩賷賱 丕賱賲卮睾賾賱 賱賱噩賱爻丞 丕賱毓丕賲丞貨 亘毓囟 丕賱賲卮睾賾賱丕鬲 丕賱賯丿賷賲丞 鬲購賰鬲卮賮 毓亘乇 毓賳賵丕賳 丕賱賳丕賮匕丞 賮賯胤 (亘丿賵賳 兀夭乇丕乇 鬲丨賰賲).
- Cider 1.x (丕賱賵丕噩賴丞 丕賱賯丿賷賲丞) 睾賷乇 賲丿毓賵賲貙 賮賯胤 2.x 賵賲丕 賮賵賯.
- 丕賱賰賱賲丕鬲 毓亘乇 丕賱廿賳鬲乇賳鬲 / AMLL / 丕賱胤賯爻 賵丕噩賴丕鬲 睾賷乇 乇爻賲賷丞 賵賯丿 鬲鬲賵賯賮 毓賳 丕賱毓賲賱.

## 鉂?丕賱兀爻卅賱丞 丕賱卮丕卅毓丞

**爻: 丕賱噩夭賷乇丞 賱丕 鬲馗賴乇責**
鬲兀賰丿 賲賳 賵噩賵丿 鬲卮睾賷賱貨 `HideWhenNoMedia` 賲賮毓賾賱 丕賮鬲乇丕囟賷賸丕貙 賵丕賱廿禺賮丕亍 亘丿賵賳 賵爻丕卅胤 胤亘賷毓賷. 卮睾賾賱 `--diagnose` 賱毓乇囟 賯丕卅賲丞 丕賱噩賱爻丕鬲.

**爻: Cider 賷毓乇囟 芦睾賷乇 賲鬲氐賱禄責**
鬲兀賰丿 賲賳 鬲賮毓賷賱 芦丕賱爻賲丕丨 亘丕賱鬲丨賰賲 丕賱禺丕乇噩賷禄 賮賷 廿毓丿丕丿丕鬲 Cider貙 賵鬲丨賯賯 賲賳 丕賱賲賳賮匕 (丕賱丕賮鬲乇丕囟賷 10767)貙 賵鬲兀賰丿 賲賳 鬲賮毓賷賱 Cider 賮賷 廿毓丿丕丿丕鬲 WinIslands.

**爻: 兀賷賯賵賳丞 丕賱毓賱亘丞 亘丕賯賷丞 亘毓丿 丕賱禺乇賵噩責**
賯丕卅賲丞 丕賱毓賱亘丞 鈫?禺乇賵噩貨 廿睾賱丕賯 賳丕賮匕丞 丕賱噩夭賷乇丞 賷禺賮賷賴丕 賮賯胤 (丕賱噩夭賷乇丞 賲賯賷賲丞 亘丕賱鬲氐賲賷賲).

---

## 馃搫 鬲乇禺賷氐 丕賱賲氐丿乇 丕賱賲賮鬲賵丨
- **🐛 Исправлено: клик по рабочему столу вызывал автоскрытие (1.2.9)**: Обнаружение полноэкранного режима теперь исключает окна рабочего стола Windows (Progman/WorkerW); клик по рабочему столу больше не вызывает автоскрытие острова.

- **馃柋 袙蟹邪懈屑芯写械泄褋褌胁懈械 懈 懈薪褌械谢谢械泻褌**: 褉邪蟹斜谢芯泻懈褉芯胁泻邪 写谢褟 锌械褉械褌邪褋泻懈胁邪薪懈褟 褋 锌褉懈谢懈锌邪薪懈械屑 泻 泻褉邪褟屑, 邪胁褌芯褋泻褉褘褌懈械 胁 锌芯谢薪芯褝泻褉邪薪薪芯屑 褉械卸懈屑械 懈 薪邪 褝泻褉邪薪械 斜谢芯泻懈褉芯胁泻懈, 斜褘褋褌褉褘械 写械泄褋褌胁懈褟 锌芯 写胁芯泄薪芯屑褍/褋褉械写薪械屑褍 泻谢懈泻褍, 泻薪芯锌泻懈 斜褘褋褌褉褘褏 写械泄褋褌胁懈泄, 锌械褉械胁邪谢芯褔薪褘泄 锌褍薪泻褌 褎邪泄谢芯胁 (锌械褉械褌邪褋泻懈胁邪薪懈械 薪邪 芯褋褌褉芯胁 懈 懈蟹 薪械谐芯 胁 写褉褍谐懈械 锌褉懈谢芯卸械薪懈褟), 褍屑薪褘泄 褉械卸懈屑 芦袧械 斜械褋锌芯泻芯懈褌褜禄 胁芯 胁褉械屑褟 蟹邪锌懈褋懈 褝泻褉邪薪邪, 锌谢邪薪芯胁芯械 锌械褉械泻谢褞褔械薪懈械 褋胁械褌谢芯泄/褌褢屑薪芯泄 褌械屑褘.
- **鈿?袩褉芯懈蟹胁芯写懈褌械谢褜薪芯褋褌褜 懈 邪胁褌芯屑邪褌懈蟹邪褑懈褟**: 褌邪泄屑械褉 Pomodoro, 蟹邪写邪褔懈, 懈褋褌芯褉懈褟 斜褍褎械褉邪 芯斜屑械薪邪, 斜褘褋褌褉褘泄 蟹邪锌褍褋泻, 薪邪锌芯屑懈薪邪薪懈褟 褉邪褋锌懈褋邪薪懈褟; 锌芯屑芯褖薪懈泻 芯褌泻谢褞褔械薪懈褟 屑懈泻褉芯褎芯薪邪 薪邪 褋芯蟹胁芯薪邪褏, 锌芯写褋泻邪蟹泻懈 蟹邪锌懈褋懈 褝泻褉邪薪邪/褋泻褉懈薪褕芯褌芯胁, 锌褉芯谐褉械褋褋 泻芯锌懈褉芯胁邪薪懈褟/蟹邪谐褉褍蟹泻懈 褎邪泄谢芯胁 薪邪 芯褋褌褉芯胁械; 谐谢芯斜邪谢褜薪褘械 谐芯褉褟褔懈械 泻谢邪胁懈褕懈 懈 写胁懈卸芯泻 锌褉邪胁懈谢.
- **馃洝 袣芯薪褎懈写械薪褑懈邪谢褜薪芯褋褌褜 懈 斜械蟹芯锌邪褋薪芯褋褌褜**: 斜械蟹 褌械谢械屑械褌褉懈懈, 斜械蟹 褉械泻谢邪屑褘, 斜械蟹 芯褌锌褉邪胁泻懈 写邪薪薪褘褏. 袩芯谢薪芯褋褌褜褞 芯褎谢邪泄薪, 泻褉芯屑械 胁泻谢褞褔褢薪薪褘褏 胁褉褍褔薪褍褞 芯薪谢邪泄薪-褌械泻褋褌芯胁 懈 锌芯谐芯写褘; 胁褋械 薪邪褋褌褉芯泄泻懈 懈 写邪薪薪褘械 褏褉邪薪褟褌褋褟 谢芯泻邪谢褜薪芯 胁 `%APPDATA%\WinIslands`.
- **馃攣 袩械褉械泻谢褞褔械薪懈械 懈褋褌芯褔薪懈泻邪 褌械泻褋褌邪 芯写薪懈屑 薪邪卸邪褌懈械屑**: 屑谐薪芯胁械薪薪芯 锌械褉械泻谢褞褔邪泄褌械褋褜 屑械卸写褍 芦袗胁褌芯 / 袥芯泻邪谢褜薪褘泄 LRC / AMLL TTML / API Cider / 袨薪谢邪泄薪-褌械泻褋褌褘禄, 褌械泻褋褌 褌械泻褍褖械泄 锌械褋薪懈 褋褉邪蟹褍 锌械褉械蟹邪谐褉褍卸邪械褌褋褟
- **馃洝锔?袗胁褌芯胁芯褋褋褌邪薪芯胁谢械薪懈械 锌芯褋谢械 褋斜芯褟**: 锌芯褋谢械 邪胁邪褉懈泄薪芯谐芯 蟹邪胁械褉褕械薪懈褟 褋谢械写褍褞褖懈泄 蟹邪锌褍褋泻 褍胁械写芯屑谢褟械褌 芯 胁芯褋褋褌邪薪芯胁谢械薪懈懈 鈥?斜械蟹 蟹邪胁懈褋邪薪懈泄, 褔褢褉薪芯谐芯 褝泻褉邪薪邪 懈 锌芯褌械褉懈 褋芯褋褌芯褟薪懈褟
- **鈴憋笍 袧邪褋褌褉邪懈胁邪械屑邪褟 写谢懈褌械谢褜薪芯褋褌褜 邪薪懈屑邪褑懈懈**: 锌芯谢蟹褍薪芯泻 写谢懈褌械谢褜薪芯褋褌懈 褉邪褋泻褉褘褌懈褟/褋胁芯褉邪褔懈胁邪薪懈褟 (300鈥?400屑褋)

## 馃敡 小斜芯褉泻邪

### 孝褉械斜芯胁邪薪懈褟
- Windows 10 1809+ / Windows 11
- .NET 8 SDK

### 小斜芯褉泻邪 懈 褌械褋褌懈褉芯胁邪薪懈械
```powershell
dotnet build WinIslands.slnx -c Release
dotnet test  WinIslands.slnx -c Release
```

### 袩褍斜谢懈泻邪褑懈褟
```powershell
# 袗胁褌芯薪芯屑薪邪褟 (胁泻谢褞褔邪械褌 褋褉械写褍 胁褘锌芯谢薪械薪懈褟 .NET 8, 褍褋褌邪薪芯胁泻邪 薪械 褌褉械斜褍械褌褋褟)
.\build\publish.ps1

# 袟邪胁懈褋懈屑邪褟 芯褌 锌谢邪褌褎芯褉屑褘 (谢褢谐泻邪褟, 薪褍卸械薪 .NET 8 Desktop Runtime)
.\build\publish.ps1 -FrameworkDependent
```
袗褉褌械褎邪泻褌褘 薪邪褏芯写褟褌褋褟 胁 `publish\win-x64\`. 小褌邪斜懈谢褜薪褘械 胁械褉褋懈懈 锌芯屑械褖邪褞褌褋褟 胁 `releases\<胁械褉褋懈褟>\win-x64\` 懈 锌械褉械懈屑械薪芯胁褘胁邪褞褌褋褟 锌芯 薪芯屑械褉褍 胁械褉褋懈懈.

### 校褋褌邪薪芯胁褖懈泻 (薪械芯斜褟蟹邪褌械谢褜薪芯)
校褋褌邪薪芯胁懈褌械 [Inno Setup 6](https://jrsoftware.org/isinfo.php), 蟹邪褌械屑 胁褘锌芯谢薪懈褌械 `iscc.exe build\release-<胁械褉褋懈褟>.iss`, 褔褌芯斜褘 锌芯谢褍褔懈褌褜 `releases\<胁械褉褋懈褟>\WinIslands-Setup-<胁械褉褋懈褟>.exe` (褍薪懈胁械褉褋邪谢褜薪褘泄 褍褋褌邪薪芯胁褖懈泻, x64 / ARM64 锌芯 邪褉褏懈褌械泻褌褍褉械).

## 馃殌 袠褋锌芯谢褜蟹芯胁邪薪懈械

1. 袟邪锌褍褋褌懈褌械 `WinIslands.exe` (邪胁褌芯蟹邪锌褍褋泻 薪邪褋褌褉邪懈胁邪械褌褋褟); 胁 褌褉械械 锌芯褟胁谢褟械褌褋褟 蟹薪邪褔芯泻; 蟹邪泻褉褘褌懈械 谐谢邪胁薪芯谐芯 芯泻薪邪 薪械 蟹邪胁械褉褕邪械褌 锌褉芯褑械褋褋 鈥?褋胁芯褉邪褔懈胁邪械褌褋褟 胁 褌褉械泄.
2. 袠谐褉邪泄褌械 谢褞斜褍褞 屑褍蟹褘泻褍: NetEase Cloud Music, QQ Music, Spotify, 芯褎懈褑懈邪谢褜薪褘泄 Apple Music 懈 写褉. 邪胁褌芯屑邪褌懈褔械褋泻懈 芯褌芯斜褉邪卸邪褞褌褋褟 褔械褉械蟹 褋懈褋褌械屑薪褍褞 屑褍谢褜褌懈屑械写懈泄薪褍褞 褋械褋褋懈褞; 写谢褟 Cider 褋屑. 薪懈卸械; 写褉褍谐懈械 锌谢械械褉褘 芯锌褉械写械谢褟褞褌褋褟 锌芯 蟹邪谐芯谢芯胁泻褍 芯泻薪邪.
3. **袣谢懈泻** 锌芯 芯褋褌褉芯胁褍 褉邪蟹胁芯褉邪褔懈胁邪械褌 锌芯谢薪褍褞 泻邪褉褌芯褔泻褍 (薪邪胁械写械薪懈械 薪械 褉邪蟹胁芯褉邪褔懈胁邪械褌): 锌械褉械褌邪褋泻懈胁邪薪懈械 seek, 褍锌褉邪胁谢械薪懈械 胁芯褋锌褉芯懈蟹胁械写械薪懈械屑, 谐褉芯屑泻芯褋褌褜, 褋懈薪褏褉芯薪懈蟹懈褉芯胁邪薪薪褘械 褌械泻褋褌褘; 械褖褢 芯写懈薪 泻谢懈泻 褋胁芯褉邪褔懈胁邪械褌.
4. 袦械薪褞 褌褉械褟: 锌芯泻邪蟹邪褌褜/褋泻褉褘褌褜, 芯褌写械谢褜薪芯械 芯泻薪芯 褌械泻褋褌邪, 邪胁褌芯蟹邪锌褍褋泻, 芦袧械 斜械褋锌芯泻芯懈褌褜禄, 锌褉芯胁械褉泻邪 芯斜薪芯胁谢械薪懈泄, 锌褉芯褋屑芯褌褉 卸褍褉薪邪谢芯胁, 薪邪褋褌褉芯泄泻懈, 胁褘褏芯写.
5. 袚谢芯斜邪谢褜薪褘械 谐芯褉褟褔懈械 泻谢邪胁懈褕懈 (胁褋械 薪邪褋褌褉邪懈胁邪褞褌褋褟): `Ctrl+Alt+P` 胁芯褋锌褉芯懈蟹胁械写械薪懈械/锌邪褍蟹邪 路 `Ctrl+Alt+鈫?鈫抈 锌褉械写褘写褍褖懈泄/褋谢械写褍褞褖懈泄 路 `Ctrl+Alt+I` 锌芯泻邪蟹邪褌褜/褋泻褉褘褌褜 路 `Ctrl+Alt+袩褉芯斜械谢` 褉邪蟹胁械褉薪褍褌褜/褋胁械褉薪褍褌褜 路 `Ctrl+袩褉芯斜械谢` 斜褘褋褌褉褘泄 蟹邪锌褍褋泻 路 `Ctrl+Alt+V` 锌邪薪械谢褜 懈褋褌芯褉懈懈 斜褍褎械褉邪 芯斜屑械薪邪.
6. 效邪褋褌褘械 邪褉谐褍屑械薪褌褘 泻芯屑邪薪写薪芯泄 褋褌褉芯泻懈: `--demo` 写械屑芯-褉械卸懈屑 路 `--diagnose` 写懈邪谐薪芯褋褌懈褔械褋泻懈泄 芯褌褔褢褌 路 `--settings` 芯褌泻褉褘褌褜 薪邪褋褌褉芯泄泻懈 锌褉懈 蟹邪锌褍褋泻械.

### 袠薪褌械谐褉邪褑懈褟 Cider
1. 袙 薪邪褋褌褉芯泄泻邪褏 Cider 胁泻谢褞褔懈褌械 芦袪邪蟹褉械褕懈褌褜 胁薪械褕薪械械 褍锌褉邪胁谢械薪懈械禄 (Allow external control).
2. 袧邪褋褌褉芯泄泻懈 WinIslands 鈫?袦械写懈邪 鈫?胁泻谢褞褔懈褌褜 Cider (锌芯褉褌 芯锌褉械写械谢褟械褌褋褟 邪胁褌芯屑邪褌懈褔械褋泻懈 `10767` 懈 褋泻邪薪懈褉褍械褌褋褟 谢芯泻邪谢褜薪芯, 谢懈斜芯 胁胁芯写懈褌褋褟 胁褉褍褔薪褍褞).
3. 袩褉懈 胁芯褋锌褉芯懈蟹胁械写械薪懈懈 懈褋褌芯褔薪懈泻 薪邪 芯褋褌褉芯胁械 鈥?`Cider`: 芯斜谢芯卸泻邪/锌褉芯谐褉械褋褋/褌械泻褋褌 懈 褍锌褉邪胁谢械薪懈械, seek, 谐褉芯屑泻芯褋褌褜.

---

## 鈿欙笍 袨锌懈褋邪薪懈械 薪邪褋褌褉芯械泻

肖邪泄谢 泻芯薪褎懈谐褍褉邪褑懈懈: `%APPDATA%\WinIslands\settings.json` (JSON; 懈蟹屑械薪械薪懈褟 胁 懈薪褌械褉褎械泄褋械 胁褋褌褍锌邪褞褌 胁 褋懈谢褍 褋褉邪蟹褍, 褝泻褋锌芯褉褌/懈屑锌芯褉褌 胁芯蟹屑芯卸薪褘).

| 袣谢褞褔 | 袩芯 褍屑芯谢褔邪薪懈褞 | 袨锌懈褋邪薪懈械 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `Language` | `zh-CN` | 携蟹褘泻 懈薪褌械褉褎械泄褋邪: `zh-CN` / `en-US` |
| `Position` | `Center` | 袩芯谢芯卸械薪懈械: `Center` 胁械褉褏 锌芯 褑械薪褌褉褍 / `Right` 胁械褉褏 褋锌褉邪胁邪 |
| `Monitor` | `Primary` | 袦芯薪懈褌芯褉: `Primary` 谐谢邪胁薪褘泄 / `All` 胁褋械 / `Index` 胁褘斜褉邪薪薪褘泄 |
| `MonitorIndex` | `0` | 袧芯屑械褉 胁褘斜褉邪薪薪芯谐芯 屑芯薪懈褌芯褉邪 |
| `OffsetX` / `OffsetY` | `0` / `8` | 小屑械褖械薪懈械 锌芯谢芯卸械薪懈褟 (锌懈泻褋械谢懈) |
| `Opacity` | `0.92` | 袧械锌褉芯蟹褉邪褔薪芯褋褌褜 0.3鈥?.0 |
| `Theme` | `Auto` | `Auto` / `Light` / `Dark` |
| `ThemePreset` | `Default` | 孝械屑邪 (18 锌褉械写褍褋褌邪薪芯胁芯泻 + Custom) |
| `AccentColor` | `#6C5CE7` | 袗泻褑械薪褌薪褘泄 褑胁械褌 `#RRGGBB` |
| `ThemeScheduledEnabled` | `false` | 袩谢邪薪芯胁芯械 锌械褉械泻谢褞褔械薪懈械 褋胁械褌谢芯泄/褌褢屑薪芯泄 褌械屑褘 (褌芯谢褜泻芯 锌褉懈 `Theme=Auto`) |
| `ThemeScheduleDarkStartHour` / `EndHour` | `19` / `7` | 效邪褋褘 薪邪褔邪谢邪/泻芯薪褑邪 褌褢屑薪芯谐芯 锌械褉懈芯写邪 |
| `FontScale` / `CornerRadius` | `1.0` / `28` | 袦邪褋褕褌邪斜 褕褉懈褎褌邪 0.8鈥?.4 / 褉邪写懈褍褋 褍谐谢芯胁 16鈥?0 |
| `AnimationStyle` | `Spring` | 孝懈锌 邪薪懈屑邪褑懈懈: `Spring` / `Soft` / `Elastic` / `Fade` |
| `CompactWidth` / `CompactHeight` | `360` / `72` | 袛谢懈薪邪 / 胁褘褋芯褌邪 胁 泻芯屑锌邪泻褌薪芯屑 褉械卸懈屑械 |
| `CompactWidthAuto` / `CompactHeightAuto` | `true` | 袗胁褌芯锌芯写斜芯褉 泻芯屑锌邪泻褌薪芯谐芯 褉邪蟹屑械褉邪 |
| `ExpandedWidth` / `MaxExpandedHeight` | `400` / `384` | 袛谢懈薪邪 / 屑邪泻褋懈屑邪谢褜薪邪褟 胁褘褋芯褌邪 胁 褉邪蟹胁褢褉薪褍褌芯屑 胁懈写械 |
| `HideWhenNoMedia` | `true` | 小泻褉褘胁邪褌褜 芯褋褌褉芯胁, 泻芯谐写邪 薪械褌 胁芯褋锌褉芯懈蟹胁械写械薪懈褟 |
| `IslandAlwaysVisible` | `false` | 袨褋褌褉芯胁 胁褋械谐写邪 胁懈写懈屑 (胁懈写卸械褌褘 锌芯泻邪蟹褘胁邪褞褌褋褟 懈 斜械蟹 胁芯褋锌褉芯懈蟹胁械写械薪懈褟) |
| `ShowWhenPaused` | `true` | 袩芯泻邪蟹褘胁邪褌褜 锌褉懈 锌邪褍蟹械 |
| `StartWithWindows` | `false` | 袗胁褌芯蟹邪锌褍褋泻 褋 Windows |
| `IsLocked` | `true` | 袟邪斜谢芯泻懈褉芯胁邪薪 (锌芯褋谢械 褉邪蟹斜谢芯泻懈褉芯胁泻懈 屑芯卸薪芯 锌械褉械褌邪褋泻懈胁邪褌褜 屑褘褕褜褞) |
| `EdgeSnapEnabled` | `true` | 袩褉懈谢懈锌邪薪懈械 泻 泻褉邪褞 / 褑械薪褌褉懈褉芯胁邪薪懈械 锌褉懈 芯褌锌褍褋泻邪薪懈懈 |
| `FullScreenAutoHideEnabled` | `true` | 袗胁褌芯褋泻褉褘褌懈械 胁 锌芯谢薪芯褝泻褉邪薪薪芯屑 褉械卸懈屑械 |
| `LockScreenAutoHideEnabled` | `true` | 袗胁褌芯褋泻褉褘褌懈械 薪邪 褝泻褉邪薪械 斜谢芯泻懈褉芯胁泻懈 (Win+L / 芯褌泻谢褞褔械薪懈械 褍写邪谢褢薪薪芯谐芯 褉邪斜芯褔械谐芯 褋褌芯谢邪), 胁芯褋褋褌邪薪芯胁谢械薪懈械 锌芯褋谢械 褉邪蟹斜谢芯泻懈褉芯胁泻懈 |
| `SingleLineMode` | `true` | 袨写薪芯褋褌褉芯褔薪褘泄 褉械卸懈屑: 胁褋械 胁懈写卸械褌褘 胁 芯写薪褍 褋褌褉芯泻褍 胁 泻芯屑锌邪泻褌薪芯屑 褋芯褋褌芯褟薪懈懈 |
| `DoubleClickAction` | `PlayPause` | 袘褘褋褌褉芯械 写械泄褋褌胁懈械 写胁芯泄薪芯谐芯 泻谢懈泻邪 锌芯 芯褋褌褉芯胁褍 |
| `MiddleClickAction` | `PlayPause` | 袘褘褋褌褉芯械 写械泄褋褌胁懈械 褋褉械写薪械谐芯 泻谢懈泻邪 锌芯 芯褋褌褉芯胁褍 |
| `CiderEnabled` / `CiderPort` | `true` / `0` | 袥芯泻邪谢褜薪褘泄 API Cider (0 = 邪胁褌芯写械褌械泻褌) |
| `OnlineLyricsEnabled` | `true` | 袨薪谢邪泄薪-褌械泻褋褌褘 (锌械褉械泻谢褞褔邪褌械谢褜 锌褉邪胁褘屑 泻谢懈泻芯屑 锌芯 芯褋褌褉芯胁褍; 薪械芯褎懈褑懈邪谢褜薪褘泄 懈薪褌械褉褎械泄褋, 褋谢械写懈褌械 蟹邪 邪胁褌芯褉褋泻懈屑懈 锌褉邪胁邪屑懈) |
| `AmllTtmlEnabled` | `true` | 袩芯褋谢芯胁薪褘械 褌械泻褋褌褘 AMLL (api.amll.dev, 薪械芯褎懈褑懈邪谢褜薪芯) |
| `KaraokeHighlight` | `true` | 袩芯褋谢芯胁薪邪褟 锌芯写褋胁械褌泻邪 泻邪褉邪芯泻械 |
| `StandaloneLyricsWindow` | `false` | 袨褌写械谢褜薪芯械 芯泻薪芯 褌械泻褋褌邪 |
| `UseSystemVolume` | `true` | 小懈褋褌械屑薪邪褟 谐褉芯屑泻芯褋褌褜 写谢褟 懈褋褌芯褔薪懈泻芯胁, 芯褌谢懈褔薪褘褏 芯褌 Cider |
| `LowBatteryThreshold` | `20` | 袩芯褉芯谐 锌褉械写褍锌褉械卸写械薪懈褟 芯 薪懈蟹泻芯屑 蟹邪褉褟写械 (%), 0 = 胁褘泻谢. |
| `DoNotDisturbManual` | `false` | 袪褍褔薪芯泄 褉械卸懈屑 芦袧械 斜械褋锌芯泻芯懈褌褜禄 |
| `DoNotDisturbEnabled` | `false` | 袩谢邪薪芯胁褘泄 褉械卸懈屑 芦袧械 斜械褋锌芯泻芯懈褌褜禄 (褌懈褏懈械 褍胁械写芯屑谢械薪懈褟 锌芯 褉邪褋锌懈褋邪薪懈褞) |
| `DoNotDisturbStartHour/Minute` | `22` / `0` | 袧邪褔邪谢芯 芦袧械 斜械褋锌芯泻芯懈褌褜禄 (褋 褌芯褔薪芯褋褌褜褞 写芯 屑懈薪褍褌褘) |
| `DoNotDisturbEndHour/Minute` | `8` / `0` | 袣芯薪械褑 芦袧械 斜械褋锌芯泻芯懈褌褜禄 (褋 褌芯褔薪芯褋褌褜褞 写芯 屑懈薪褍褌褘) |
| `IslandApiEnabled` / `IslandApiPort` | `true` / `9840` | 袩械褉械泻谢褞褔邪褌械谢褜 / 锌芯褉褌 API Island |
| `IslandApiToken` | `""` | 袧械芯斜褟蟹邪褌械谢褜薪褘泄 褌芯泻械薪 API Island |
| `IslandApiDefaultDuration` | `30` | 袛谢懈褌械谢褜薪芯褋褌褜 锌芯泻邪蟹邪 锌芯 褍屑芯谢褔邪薪懈褞 (褋械泻褍薪写褘) |
| `WaveStyle` | `Bars` | 小褌懈谢褜 蟹胁褍泻芯胁芯泄 胁芯谢薪褘: `Bars` / `Spectrum` / `Ring` / `Particles` |
| `WidgetOrder` | `Time,Weather,...` | 袩芯褉褟写芯泻 胁懈写卸械褌芯胁 (泻谢褞褔懈 褔械褉械蟹 蟹邪锌褟褌褍褞) |
| `Components` | 芯斜褗械泻褌 | 肖谢邪卸泻懈 芦斜械蟹 屑褍蟹褘泻懈 / 褋 屑褍蟹褘泻芯泄禄 写谢褟 泻邪卸写芯谐芯 胁懈写卸械褌邪 |
| `MediaApps` | `[]` | 袙泻谢褞褔械薪懈械/芯褌泻谢褞褔械薪懈械 懈 锌褉懈芯褉懈褌械褌 锌谢械械褉芯胁 |
| `Rules` | `[]` | 袩褉邪胁懈谢邪 邪胁褌芯屑邪褌懈蟹邪褑懈懈 (褍褋谢芯胁懈械 + 写械泄褋褌胁懈械) |
| `ReduceMotion` | `false` | 校屑械薪褜褕懈褌褜 邪薪懈屑邪褑懈褞 (写芯褋褌褍锌薪芯褋褌褜/褝泻芯薪芯屑懈褟 褝薪械褉谐懈懈) |
| `GlobalHotkeysEnabled` | `true` | 袩械褉械泻谢褞褔邪褌械谢褜 谐谢芯斜邪谢褜薪褘褏 谐芯褉褟褔懈褏 泻谢邪胁懈褕 |
| `LowPowerMode` | `false` | 协薪械褉谐芯褋斜械褉械谐邪褞褖懈泄 褉械卸懈屑: 褋薪懈卸械薪懈械 褔邪褋褌芯褌褘 胁 锌褉芯褋褌芯械, 褍锌褉芯褖褢薪薪褘械 邪薪懈屑邪褑懈懈 |

## 馃彎 API Island (懈薪褌械谐褉邪褑懈褟 褋褌芯褉芯薪薪懈褏 锌褉懈谢芯卸械薪懈泄)

袥褞斜芯械 袩袨 屑芯卸械褌 芯褌锌褉邪胁谢褟褌褜 懈薪褎芯褉屑邪褑懈褞 薪邪 Dynamic Island 褔械褉械蟹 谢芯泻邪谢褜薪褘泄 HTTP / WebSocket-懈薪褌械褉褎械泄褋, 泻邪泻 懈薪褌械谐褉邪褑懈褟 Island 褋褌芯褉芯薪薪懈褏 锌褉懈谢芯卸械薪懈泄 胁 iOS.

| 袦械褌芯写 | 袩褍褌褜 | 袨锌懈褋邪薪懈械 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| POST | `/v1/island/push` | 袨褌锌褉邪胁懈褌褜 / 芯斜薪芯胁懈褌褜 泻邪褉褌芯褔泻褍 |
| POST | `/v3/island/push` | 袧邪写屑薪芯卸械褋褌胁芯 v1: 懈蟹芯斜褉邪卸械薪懈械 / 邪薪懈屑懈褉芯胁邪薪薪褘泄 锌褉芯谐褉械褋褋 / heartbeat |
| PATCH | `/v3/island/push/{id}` | 效邪褋褌懈褔薪芯械 芯斜薪芯胁谢械薪懈械 |
| DELETE | `/v1/island/push/{id}` | 校写邪谢懈褌褜 泻邪褉褌芯褔泻褍 |
| GET | `/v1/island/active` | 袟邪锌褉芯褋 邪泻褌懈胁薪芯泄 泻邪褉褌芯褔泻懈 |
| GET | `/v3/ws` | 袛胁褍薪邪锌褉邪胁谢械薪薪褘泄 泻邪薪邪谢 WebSocket |
| GET | `/v1/health` | 袩褉芯胁械褉泻邪 褉邪斜芯褌芯褋锌芯褋芯斜薪芯褋褌懈 |

袩芯写写械褉卸泻邪: 蟹邪谐芯谢芯胁芯泻/褌械泻褋褌/懈泻芯薪泻邪/锌芯写蟹邪谐芯谢芯胁芯泻, 锌褉芯谐褉械褋褋, 泻薪芯锌泻懈 (芯褌泻褉褘褌褜 褋褋褘谢泻褍 / 蟹邪锌褍褋褌懈褌褜 锌褉芯谐褉邪屑屑褍 / 胁褘锌芯谢薪懈褌褜 泻芯屑邪薪写褍 / 芯斜褉邪褌薪褘泄 胁褘蟹芯胁 notify), 锌芯谢褟 胁胁芯写邪, 懈蟹芯斜褉邪卸械薪懈褟, 邪薪懈屑懈褉芯胁邪薪薪褘泄 锌褉芯谐褉械褋褋, 锌褉芯写谢械薪懈械 锌芯 heartbeat, 褋胁械褌谢邪褟/褌褢屑薪邪褟 褌械屑邪, 薪邪褋褌褉邪懈胁邪械屑褘泄 邪泻褑械薪褌薪褘泄 褑胁械褌, 芯褔械褉械写褜 锌褉懈芯褉懈褌械褌芯胁. **袨褌锌褉邪胁泻邪 薪械 屑械薪褟械褌 褕懈褉懈薪褍 芯褋褌褉芯胁邪**.

袩芯谢薪邪褟 写芯泻褍屑械薪褌邪褑懈褟: [docs/IslandAPI.md](docs/IslandAPI.md)

## 馃洝 袣芯薪褎懈写械薪褑懈邪谢褜薪芯褋褌褜 懈 斜械蟹芯锌邪褋薪芯褋褌褜

- **袘械蟹 褌械谢械屑械褌褉懈懈, 斜械蟹 褉械泻谢邪屑褘, 斜械蟹 芯褌锌褉邪胁泻懈 写邪薪薪褘褏**. 袣褉芯屑械 胁泻谢褞褔褢薪薪褘褏 胁褉褍褔薪褍褞 芦芯薪谢邪泄薪-褌械泻褋褌芯胁禄 懈 芦锌芯谐芯写褘禄, 锌褉懈谢芯卸械薪懈械 薪械 胁褘锌芯谢薪褟械褌 褋械褌械胁褘褏 蟹邪锌褉芯褋芯胁.
- 袝写懈薪褋褌胁械薪薪褘械 褋械褌械胁褘械 褋褑械薪邪褉懈懈: 蟹邪谐褉褍蟹泻邪 芯斜谢芯卸械泻 Cider, 锌芯褋谢芯胁薪褘械 褌械泻褋褌褘 AMLL (api.amll.dev), 芯薪谢邪泄薪-褌械泻褋褌褘 懈 锌芯谐芯写邪 锌芯褋谢械 胁泻谢褞褔械薪懈褟 (Open-Meteo).
- 袙褋械 写邪薪薪褘械 褏褉邪薪褟褌褋褟 谢芯泻邪谢褜薪芯 胁 `%APPDATA%\WinIslands\`; 卸褍褉薪邪谢褘 蟹邪锌懈褋褘胁邪褞褌 褌芯谢褜泻芯 谢芯泻邪谢褜薪褍褞 懈薪褎芯褉屑邪褑懈褞 芯 褉邪斜芯褌械.

- 袩芯褋谢芯胁薪芯械 泻邪褉邪芯泻械 蟹邪胁懈褋懈褌 芯褌 懈褋褌芯褔薪懈泻邪 懈 锌褉芯谐褉械褋褋邪 褌械泻褋褌邪: 锌褉懈 锌芯褋谢芯胁薪芯泄 褕泻邪谢械 胁褉械屑械薪懈 AMLL TTML / LRC 鈥?锌芯写褋胁械褌泻邪 锌芯 褋谢芯胁邪屑, 懈薪邪褔械 懈褋锌芯谢褜蟹褍械褌褋褟 锌芯写褋胁械褌泻邪 胁褋械泄 褋褌褉芯泻懈.

- Cider 1.x (褋褌邪褉褘泄 API) 薪械 锌芯写写械褉卸懈胁邪械褌褋褟, 褌芯谢褜泻芯 2.x 懈 胁褘褕械.

## 馃敡 Compila莽茫o

### Requisitos
- Windows 10 1809+ / Windows 11
- SDK .NET 8

### Compilar e testar
```powershell
dotnet build WinIslands.slnx -c Release
dotnet test  WinIslands.slnx -c Release
```

### Publicar
```powershell
# Autossuficiente (inclui o runtime .NET 8, sem instala莽茫o)
.\build\publish.ps1

# Dependente de framework (leve, requer .NET 8 Desktop Runtime)
.\build\publish.ps1 -FrameworkDependent
```
Os artefatos ficam em `publish\win-x64\`. As vers玫es est谩veis v茫o para `releases\<vers茫o>\win-x64\` e s茫o renomeadas conforme o n煤mero da vers茫o.

### Instalador (opcional)

## 馃彎 API Island (integra莽茫o de terceiros)

Qualquer software pode enviar informa莽玫es ao Dynamic Island pela interface HTTP / WebSocket local, como a integra莽茫o Island de apps de terceiros no iOS.

| M茅todo | Caminho | Descri莽茫o |
| --- | --- | --- |
| POST | `/v1/island/push` | Enviar / atualizar um cart茫o |
| POST | `/v3/island/push` | Superconjunto do v1: imagem / progresso animado / heartbeat |
| PATCH | `/v3/island/push/{id}` | Atualiza莽茫o parcial |
| DELETE | `/v1/island/push/{id}` | Remover um cart茫o |
| GET | `/v1/island/active` | Consultar o cart茫o ativo |
| GET | `/v3/ws` | Canal WebSocket bidirecional |
| GET | `/v1/health` | Verifica莽茫o de integridade |

Suporta: t铆tulo/corpo/铆cone/subt铆tulo, progresso, bot玫es (abrir link / iniciar programa / executar comando / retorno notify), campos de entrada, imagens, progresso animado, renova莽茫o por heartbeat, temas claro/escuro, cor de destaque personalizada, fila de prioridade. **O envio n茫o altera a largura da ilha**.

Documenta莽茫o completa: [docs/IslandAPI.md](docs/IslandAPI.md)

## 馃洝 Privacidade e seguran莽a

- **Sem telemetria, sem an煤ncios, sem envio de dados**. Exceto por "letras on-line" e "clima" ativados manualmente, o aplicativo n茫o faz nenhuma solicita莽茫o de rede.
- 脷nicas conex玫es de rede: download de capas do Cider, letras palavra por palavra AMLL (api.amll.dev), letras on-line e clima ap贸s ativa莽茫o (Open-Meteo).
- Todos os dados s茫o armazenados localmente em `%APPDATA%\WinIslands\`; os logs registram apenas informa莽玫es locais de execu莽茫o.

## 鈿狅笍 Limita莽玫es conhecidas

- O karaok锚 palavra por palavra depende da fonte e do progresso das letras: com linha do tempo palavra por palavra AMLL TTML / LRC, destaque por palavra; caso contr谩rio, usa destaque da frase inteira.
- Alguns players relatam progresso que recua (ex.: Cider/SMTC relatam 0 brevemente): a prote莽茫o de posi莽茫o ignora recuos instant芒neos.
- A cobertura SMTC depende de o player registrar a sess茫o de m铆dia global; alguns players antigos s贸 s茫o detectados por t铆tulo de janela (sem bot玫es de controle).
- Cider 1.x (API antiga) n茫o suportado, apenas 2.x e superior.
- Letras on-line / AMLL / clima s茫o interfaces n茫o oficiais e podem parar de funcionar.
